- The repaired journey observes Ben immediately after reload, makes Ada rename Ben's bound document, verifies its received `meta` frame and completed metadata-only bridge delivery, and checks all invariants before injecting the positive-control replacement. The replacement preserves the generation, is required to trigger invariant 4, and is restored before teardown.
- Deviation: this is a test-gate repair, so the red proof is a deliberate same-generation DOM replacement in the real journey, not a product mutation. No M1 binding files were edited; current M1 changes arrived only through the required merge. No local tests, stack or browser were run.
- P2 follow-ups remain scoped separately under the owner's review rule: T2.1 channel hardening (fetch deadline, visibility preserving the pong deadline, retries respecting vault-listing prerequisites); reconciliation after dropped notifications and targeted listing queries; T2.3 trash/restore publication and j05 delivery; T2.4/T2.5 agent grants/removals and session-ended. The coordinator still owns the inherited `CI_CLOSED_MILESTONE` policy.

- T2.2 checker P2s: include doc and folder moves (nested docs too) in the awaited live-access recheck → T2.5; folder trash should wait for unacked edits (A§10.6) and offer a retry after a 503 from a DocDO close → T2.3; a refused drag-and-drop move only logs and snaps back, with no visible refusal → T2.2 follow-up in the vendored notes panel; j06 has no folder-subtree move leg (drag a folder into another and check the paths) → T2.2 follow-up.

### T1.9 — decorator text registers

- Tests-first [checks 37170094056](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37170094056) reproduced lost concurrent edits for code, HTML and formula payloads; [Chromium 37170314835](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37170314835) reproduced the missing peer text in an open code field.
- The three payloads use `Y.Text` entries under stable node register identities. Setters use the title's minimal text diff; fields are excluded from whole-value attributes. The client undo scope and DocDO mirror share the mechanism, and converter getters preserve markdown bytes. Legacy V1 attributes migrate in place on load without replacing nodes or deleting their original attributes.
- Code and HTML drafts publish live, remap remote caret positions, and merge composition drafts. Formula popovers receive remote source edits and use collaborative undo. The existing node views call a shared field hook directly; the Formula node is not part of the extracted view registry. This is the task-specific alternative to A§10.10's proposed view wrapper. Deviation 21 records live field closing behavior.
- Validation covers live V1 editor replication for all three fields, concurrent setters, own-origin undo/redo, persistence, import identities, legacy migration and export parity. The j01 register journeys exercise code merging/undo/redo/reload, HTML merging/undo, and formula peer updates/undo in CI. Final head and run URLs are recorded in the uncommitted `.codex-result.json`.
- No separate `docs/briefs/T1.9.md` exists; BUILDPLAN's entry and the supplied implementer brief define the scope. No local tests, typechecks or lint ran. Browser Automation is unavailable to this Codex implementer as the brief states; the independent checker owns the real browser pass. No local stack or browser was started. No T1.9-owned P2 follow-up was listed.
- The first complete implementation check is green at `0f98ba9` ([37171579169](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37171579169)). Code and formula journeys pass in both engines. The HTML journey exposed a pre-existing preview-error layer intercepting Edit HTML; the viewport now isolates its layers so the existing header remains clickable. Moss’s `SharedPreviewFrame` and `MediaNodeHeader` at the pin put the header before a same-level preview error overlay; the web’s unavailable native screenshot consistently exposes that state. This small HTML-only repair is required to exercise the register through its UI.

### T1.9 — register refresh commit isolation

- Tests-first [checks 37173699258](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37173699258) reproduced lost code-language and formula-result writes in non-discrete updates. The existing [formula-to-color journey 37173699220](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37173699220) failed at the reported missing peer color chip. [Checks 37173874624](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37173874624) proved all three regression assertions red, including an authored update arriving after a remote register refresh begins.
- Refresh must wait while Lexical has a pending update, retry after its commit, and commit its own excluded cache writes discretely. This prevents collaboration tags from swallowing either an earlier or a later authored update. Regression assertions cover peer state, persisted state, undo and background-writer eligibility; final verification uses the full CI lane in both engines.
- Reference comparison: glyphdown stores the body in one `Y.Text` (S-gd §0), so has no Lexical register cache. Moss at the pin writes code and formula fields directly (`CodeBlockNode.tsx:535`, `FormulaNode.tsx:252–260`). Our register cache introduced the extra tagged update; the fix isolates it without changing stored fields or node identities.
- Deviations: the existing j01 formula-to-color leg is reused as the P1 browser regression. No separate task brief exists beyond BUILDPLAN and `.codex-brief.md`. The optional local browser pass remains with the independent checker because Browser Automation is unavailable in this Codex environment, as the brief states. No local tests, typechecks or lint ran, and no stack or browser was started. The three P2 findings remain recorded follow-ups under the owner's P0/P1-only review rule; this repair changes only commit isolation. Exact final-head evidence is in `.codex-result.json`.

### T1.9 — unique register ids for live imports

- The checker reproduced two editors pasting the same markdown into one empty note: both minted the deterministic `import:` id, Y.Map kept one `Y.Text`, and the two blocks stayed linked. [Checks 37176278081](https://github.com/brsbl/moss-multi/actions/runs/37176278081) proved it red for all three registers.
- `$assignRegisterIds` now mints `crypto.randomUUID()` in a live editor. Only the DocDO mirror (`bindRegisters(..., { serializedImports: true })`, one serialized writer) and unbound converters keep repeatable import ids (A§10.10).
- T2.2s verified: moving a note or folder is the vault owner's alone; editors (by grant or share link) can still create and rename but are refused a move and are not offered a drag.
- 2026-10-04 — M3 started on branch m3 (stacked on m2) in parallel with M2, at the owner's request to up the pace; the local stack cap rose from 2 to 5. Claude builds, Codex reviews.
- 2026-10-04 — M4 started on branch m4 (stacked on m3) with the comments (T4.0) and suggestions (T5.0) design reviews, run early to use the Claude week before its Sunday reset.

### From M2's Slop Cop review (PR #4 @ af17d0a, 2026-10-05)
- Re-check current authority inside editor and manager REST writes (folder create/rename, note insert, link revoke) (apps/web/src/api/folders.ts:162-166 (createFolder INSERT), folders.ts:222-223 (rename-only). Add a current-authority predicate to each write. Preserve user, agent-owner, direct-grant, inherited-grant and presented-editor-link authority, since editsFolder alone covers only users. Treat zero changed rows as a fres
- Send the initial presence snapshot only if the validated attachment allows presence (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:318-319 (onConnect)). Read attachmentOf(connection)?.presenceAllowed after validation, or send the snapshot through the existing presence sender.
- Folder rename response reveals a parent outside the link's scope (apps/web/src/api/folders.ts:141-144, 260 (folderRecord returned by updateFolder)). When the caller's access is linkOnly and the parent is outside their link, omit parentId or rewrite it relative to the link scope.
- A move can overwrite a newer filename from the title projection (apps/web/src/api/folders.ts:395-410 (moveDoc)). Add filename = <read value> to the UPDATE's WHERE clause and re-read and recompute on a conflict. Alternatively, write filename only when the move actually has to rename around a collision, and compare it there.
- Per-note serial reach reads on folder moves (packages/sync/src/fanout.ts:162-185, called from folders.ts:214 and from kickLosses). Compute reach for the whole subtree with set-based queries keyed by doc ID (json_each), and keep the before/after comparison.
- Refreshes filtered by ids still do full-workspace work (apps/web/src/api/workspace.ts:165-166, 225-235). Add an ids-aware path that narrows doc and Trash queries before building rows, and compute counts in one pass.
- reapDeadInvites re-checks every open invite in the database (apps/web/src/api/access.ts:105-108). Scope the reaper to the affected targets and subtree, or to the affected inviter for grant changes, while keeping death synchronous and permanent.
- T2.S2 (Folder-link listing at a fixed cost), checker: the folder-link listing authorizes docs against a folder tree read one query earlier (apps/web/src/api/workspace.ts:113-154), so a concurrent move of a subfolder out of the linked root could leak one listing's metadata for a note in it; read the subtree and docs in one statement or re-check the chain for docs whose folder changed.
- T3.R (Restack m3 on m2), checker: a chart or canvas whose payload arrives empty stays read-only for good, because readiness still requires the payload map to have size > 0 after arrival (an empty legacy map, a fresh id from the gate's rename, or a payload dropped by retention), and the setter is a silent no-op on it; not a regression from T3.R.

### From M1's Slop Cop review (PR #2 @ ff9570f, 2026-10-05)
- REST rename and create's initial title skip the document size cap (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:263-265 (create), 277-281 (renameTitle)). Build the title change on a mirror doc, admit the resulting state plus payload bytes, and only then apply it. Return the existing doc-cap refusal.
- A failed D1 title projection is never retried or reconciled (packages/sync/src/doc/projections.ts:61-80). Keep pending title work separate from touch errors. Retry with backoff, and reconcile after wake.
- Concurrent share raises can lower a just-granted higher role (apps/web/src/api/members.ts:115-123). Make the UPDATE conditional on the stored role being lower than the new role, for example WHERE role is in the lower roles. Re-read the stored role to build the response.
- Folder discovery reads every folder from every tenant on each workspace poll (apps/web/src/api/access.ts:114-134 (accessibleFolders)). Start from the caller's owned folders and grant targets, then walk only the relevant descendants and the ancestors needed for each.
- A payload over 256 KiB is fully re-compacted on every later edit (packages/sync/src/payloads.ts:442-460). Count bytes written since the last compaction separately from the snapshot size, and reset that count after compacting.
- Layout persistence walks the whole tree and writes localStorage on every editor update (apps/web/src/host/collab/layout-local.ts:39-76). Skip updates that do not touch tables or tabs, write only values that changed, and batch writes into one, flushing on teardown.
- The ack ledger keeps acknowledged writes during a continuous editing stream (apps/web/src/host/collab/acks.ts:48-57). On each ack, drop the writes it fully covers, and keep only those still outstanding.
- Expanding YAML aliases in imported frontmatter is unbounded (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:257). Before normalizing, detect aliases and cycles or count expanded nodes against a budget, and refuse import when the budget is exceeded.
- T5.1 checker P2 (`suggest_leases` gains `fork_id` with no ALTER migration; downgraded from Codex P1): a pre-existing table without `fork_id` would fail every lease insert; no such persisted state exists yet, but add an idempotent nullable ALTER before any deploy.
- T5.1 checker P2 (the code-block wedge's hover preview cannot be reached with the mouse): in Edit mode the wedge for a suggested payload edit sits under moss's block gap-cursor overlay (z-20), so the proposed-code preview never shows on hover; the inline text preview box is also too narrow and wraps one word per line.
- T5.1 checker P2 (Suggest mode turns off all of MathCalculationPlugin, so a typed formula cannot be committed): `backgroundWriters=false` gates the whole plugin, including the Space and Enter commit commands; the census only pastes formula markup.
- T5.1 checker P2 (routing and caret capture treat a continuation lease's accepted prefix as pending): `ownClients` works per client, so after an accept during an outage Backspace on the accepted prefix takes the fork's native-delete path instead of a strike, and caret capture skips that prefix; paint already checks per item against B's state vector.
- T5.3 checker P2 ('Copy suggested text' copies insertion history and trims whitespace): suggestedText joins every inserted ContentString from the recorded updates, ignoring later deletions and document order, then trims; a suggestion that inserts then replaces text copies both versions, and code indentation is lost.
- T5.3 checker P2 (review hit-testing includes the accepted prefix of a continuation record): the click predicate matches every item from the record's clients, so clicking unpainted accepted text can open the continuation's card and stop a link there from navigating; painting already uses the body-clock boundary.
- T5.3 checker P2 (anonymous share-link readers get 401 on preview): handleSuggestion refuses anonymous principals before resolving the share token, so a reader on a valid link cannot load a preview.
- T5.3 checker P2 (no real-DocDO regression test for re-authorizing a review after access changes): REST tests use a stubbed DocDO and the harness DocDO has a null access checker, so review after revocation, demotion, session end or a revoked share token is uncovered; Codex found no bypass.
- T5.3 checker P2 (red run shows some negative controls failing only because a symbol was missing): in red run 37517383678 five of ten failures came from rowSegments not existing yet and the trim negative control passed, so the red run does not show both semantic negative controls failing for the intended reason.
- T5.4 checker P2 (the fuzz ingest leg mostly stops at the lease refusal): ingestLeg (packages/sync/src/suggest/fuzz.test.ts) builds frames under the fixture's leased client but sends them with a fresh lease, so most struct-bearing frames are refused as 'lease' before placement and record append, and only throws are checked; build frames under the granted lease and assert on the results.
- T5.4 checker P2 (the broken-record exclusion check reads only the body state vector): the F check compares only Y.encodeStateVector and the C check only the root value, so a broken record leaking deletes or payload-only changes into F or C would go unnoticed; also compare delete sets and payload states, with negative controls that leak only deletes or only payloads.
- T5.3s checker P2 (preview cost is quadratic when repeated delete-only ops cover the record's own inserted structs; coordinator-downgraded from the checker's P1): with the pinned yjs a record under the 256 KiB cap takes 391 ms to preview and doubling the record takes about 4x as long; bounded by the record cap, so a linear-cost goal miss, not an outage. Carried to the M5 Slop Cop for a severity decision and a fix there.
- T5.3s checker P2 (doc-do.ts #ack builds its acked map keyed by payload id on a plain object, outside the suggest/ scope): carried to the M5 Slop Cop.
- T5.3s checker P2 (lib0 readAny decodes a `__proto__` key in a forged Any object by changing that object's prototype; dependency): carried to the M5 Slop Cop; every consumer of decoded ContentAny must read own keys only.
- T5.3s checker P2 (strike-then-join keeps the struck character after accept; pre-existing on m5): Lexical re-creates the joined text as new items, so the strike is lost from what the reviewer accepts.
- T5.3s checker P2 (whole added or removed nodes drop enclosing map-field names from the visible card row).
- T5.S3 (No long-run work test for forward Delete) checker P2: the instrumented tests cover only Backspace past a long struck run; forward Delete uses the same per-node cache in `$routeChar` (suggest/routing.ts) and was fast in the browser (99 ms over 20,002 struck units), but no CI test bounds its work.
- T5.S3 (Struck runs over 100,000 characters still end at the loop guard; pre-existing) checker P2: `guard < 100_000` in `$routeChar` counts struck graphemes, so a longer run in one node falls through to the native handler and the server refuses the write visibly; the guard could count nodes or jump contiguous struck intervals.
- T3.S13 (Payload frames leave nothing parked) checker P2: a near-cap self-parented or parent-cycle frame throws inside the state-cap estimate (`stateBytesAfter`, packages/sync/src/doc/admission.ts:174-182) and is silently dropped by doc-do.ts:1029 with no 4409 and no ack; refuse it `unresolved` for parity (forged frames only, nothing parked).
- T3.S13 (Payload frames leave nothing parked) checker P2: rows compacted before the fix may already hold parked structs, so `applyFrame` (payloads.ts:338-355) blames the next honest frame with 4409 until a threshold compaction; purge pending state when the rows load.
- T3.S13 (Payload frames leave nothing parked) checker P2: `payloads.test` first inspects the rows after a second compaction (so it does not isolate compact-after-purge), never gives `raw()` delete ranges (pendingDs untested), and its throwing fixtures use higher client ids (partial apply untested); check the rows right after the refused frame and add those cases.
- T3.B5 (Discovery scales with the caller) checker P2: id-filtered refreshes still read all of the caller's direct doc grants: managedTrash's docGrants and folderLinkListing's docMembers queries (apps/web/src/api/workspace.ts) filter only by principal, so an ids=<one> refresh reads one grant row per note shared directly with the caller (scaling gap, not an access regression) → apply the ids predicate to both grant reads and add unrelated direct grants to the discovery-scale row-count fixture.
- T3.S16 (Bounded backlinks) checker P2: the harness test measures returned rows and the query plan, not SQLite rowsRead: `search.test` (packages/sync/test/harness/search.test.ts:69) counts `cursor.toArray().length` after DISTINCT and the node:sqlite FakeState has no rowsRead counter, so the bound rests on the EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN assertions (links via links_src, driven by json_each, never scanned) → add a workerd test comparing a consumed cursor's rowsRead before and after adding 50,000 inaccessible sources.
- T3.S18 (Non-finite property values are changes) checker P2: the finding's end-to-end push coverage is deferred because packages/sync/src/push.ts and the CLI arrive in m7; that push carries the fix is inferred from source (push.ts:258 calls importFrontmatter), not run → T7.2 red-first test.
- T3.S18 (Non-finite property values are changes) checker P2: `stable()` still treats -0 and 0 as equal, and `same()` in apps/web/src/host/collab/frontmatter-binding.ts:29 still compares with JSON.stringify, so null, NaN and ±Infinity look equal when deciding whether an open property draft is held (no current UI path produces these values; Properties edits strings) → frontmatter follow-up: share the tagged equality with the binding and decide -0.
- T3.S14 (Bounded full-state answers) checker P2: anonymous reconnects get a fresh answer budget each time: each anonymous socket's budget is deleted on close (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts budgetKey/socketKey, onClose), so a link holder that keeps reconnecting is limited only by its connect rate, not ANSWER_BUDGET.
- T3.S14 (Bounded full-state answers) checker P2: answer cost counts Yjs clocks, not bytes (missingShare, packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:214-228), so a vector claiming every clock but a large ContentAny's gets a near-full answer at the 1/32 floor (about 256 large answers per burst instead of 8; still bounded by the 1/32 and 1/4096 floors).
- T3.S14 (Bounded full-state answers) checker P2: the anonymous budget is keyed by the client-chosen connection.id (`_pk`), so two live sockets with the same `_pk` share a budget and either one closing deletes the other's; key it by the Connection object, as the waiting map and write-rate accounting do.
- T3.S20 (One notice when access ends) checker P2: `DocSession.end()` always settles the view-only notice, and the refusal store (refusal.ts) is one tab-wide message matched on text, so note B ending within 4 s of note A's demotion in a split pane clears A's still-accurate notice (cosmetic) → refusal follow-up: scope the settle to the note that raised the notice.
- T4.B2 (Comment anchors, frame guard coverage and excluded fields) checker P2: budget-exhausted predecessor-block descents inside `prev()` are not memoized (B255 residual). `#reach` stores a step only after `prev()` returns, and `prev()` can throw OverBudget partway through a left sibling block's children (anchor-frame.ts:277-288), so a frame that deletes a block with more than WALK_BUDGET child structs before a block with many comments redoes the same budget-sized scan per comment; still bounded at one WALK_BUDGET per comment, so a missed optimisation rather than a regression; the 9,000-item test covers only a gap inside one paragraph.
- T4.B2 (Comment anchors, frame guard coverage and excluded fields) checker P2: no red evidence for the B265 workerd j15-comments-guard fixtures with the guard or purge disabled; tests-first run 38036985164 skipped e2e and its failures came from the unit assertions, so add a run that shows those workerd fixtures failing with the guard or purge off (the Node harness already has red coverage for the same fixtures).
- T4.B1 (B260 lacks executed negative controls) checker P2: `body-cap.harness.test.ts` passed on its first run (4079877f) and its smaller-cap control is only an in-test byte comparison against MARKDOWN_ONLY_BODY_MAX_BYTES; no remote-CI run shows the case failing with the old stub, an invalid sidecar or the restored smaller cap (evidence gap; backlog P3, follow-up only).
- T3.B25 (Anonymous answer budgets survive reconnects) checker P2: the 'signed-in readers keep one budget across sockets' test in `packages/sync/test/harness/doc-do.test.ts` cannot fail if each socket gets its own budget (CAP+2 = 5 sockets, one empty-vector step 1 each, at most BUDGET.docs+1 = 5 answers allowed), so it does not guard the one-budget clause; the code is correct (`budgetKey` uses principalId for signed-in principals, doc-do.ts:201) — test-evidence gap only.
- T3.B18 (Selection export across blocks) checker P2: `$atStartOf` (apps/web/src/host/selection.ts:223-230) checks only textContent and media selectors, so a prefix holding only a `<br>` (a paragraph or list item starting with a LineBreakNode) counts as 'at start' and the endWhole branch (:431-434) or `$endsAtItemStart` (:237, :264) can drop a block or item the user entered.
- T3.B18 (Selection export across blocks) checker P2: `$lineIn` counts nested decorators by equal getCode/getRawHtml output (selection.ts:281-292), so an unfenced raw-HTML blockquote with the same text as a code block's draft shifts the ordinal, fenceIn returns null, and the selection widens to the whole tab group (contrived; degrades to the whole block).

- T3.B19 (Auth and sign-out flows) checker P2: the full lane was green on the task head f173cf6e, not the m3 head; closed by integration CI on the merged m3 SHA.
- T3.B21 (Converter measurement gates) checker P2: the warm-search recovery check in `searchBudgetProblems` (scripts/measure-converter.mjs) only compares the post-adversarial snippet with the baseline, so two equally empty, null or missing snippets pass; the worker checks the hit count but not the snippet → assert the known snippet for the warm fixture.
- T3.B21 (Converter measurement gates) checker P2: the B337 near-boundary RSS samples in `measure-converter.test` are illustrative, not recorded from CI, and the sustained-growth control runs only at predicate level on made-up arrays (three unchanged remote SP2 runs at one SHA grew -9.2, 12.2 and 10.0 MB against the 64 MB budget) → add a remote control that allocates real memory through the sampler.
- T3.B6 (Folder, trash and vault write races and authority) checker P2: link revocation can make the rename response disclose the link root's parent — `updateFolder` decides whether to hide `parentId` with a fresh `liveLink()` lookup after the write, so a revoke between the rename UPDATE and that lookup returns the link root's real (opaque) parentId (narrow race) → keep the link scope that authorized the request, or fail closed.
- T3.B2 (Serialized, admitted title writes) checker P2: a note opened from Trash with no frontmatter shows an empty, permanently busy Properties panel: the new gate (`frontmatter === null && !bound`) renders an empty aria-busy div, and the read-only Trash view never makes a collaborative binding, so it never leaves that state (before, it showed 'No properties yet') → tell 'content not loaded yet' apart from 'collab binding' on the Trash read path.
- T3.B2 (Serialized, admitted title writes) checker P2: the j02 IME test cannot catch an early commit of composing text: it types and acknowledges the whole name before compositionstart, sends composition events with empty data, and its 'nothing committed early' check reads the same DOM text, so pushing draft composing input into the shared title before compositionend would still pass (the focus and preventDefault checks do work and were red before the fix).
- T3.B22 (CI and local stack tooling) checker P2: `drainUnreadBody` (apps/web/src/worker/drain.ts, called from server.ts) awaits `request.arrayBuffer()` with no deadline, so a sender that declares a small Content-Length and holds the body open delays its own refusal (only its own request; no lock or DO state held) → add a short drain deadline.
- T3.B22 (CI and local stack tooling) checker P2: the wrangler patch stamps a pooled connection idle when response headers arrive, but kj returns it to the pool only after the body is consumed, so after a long streamed body (R2 media) a POST can still land on a connection about to idle out (local and CI tooling only) → stamp idle at body completion.
- T3.B22 (CI and local stack tooling) checker P2: `stack.mjs` writes secrets.env before spawn, so a launch that throws before `waitServing` never runs `removeSecretEnv` and leaves the 0600 file in the run dir (local hygiene) → remove it on every launch failure path.
- T3.B22 (CI and local stack tooling) checker P2: the red run 38038553390 failed the B063 proxy, B065 stack and B042 --write legs on harness errors (headers.getAll, unexported wranglerArgs and main) rather than the named defects; the assertions would fail against the old code, so this is a process gap only.
- T3.B13 (Editor vendor seams) checker P2: IME cancel can remove a peer's in-flight text in the gap paragraph — the ime-cancel handler (DecoratorBlockPlugin.tsx:700-718) judges emptiness from local state only, so a peer's keystrokes still in flight into that transient paragraph are dropped with it (one round-trip window during an open composition).
- T3.B13 (Editor vendor seams) checker P2: B346 ships the binding fix only; the brief's offscreen-rendering fix (content-visibility/containment) and its input-to-paint gate are not delivered — j01-large's input-to-paint and long-task limits (240/260/1000 ms) also pass unpatched and the leg has no comment-anchor assertion; viewport-bounded rendering is deferred to the owner.
- T3.B13 (Editor vendor seams) checker P2: a `==` inside inline code counts as a highlight opener (highlightRunStarts, formula-runtime.ts:655-660 ignores codeRanges), so after `a==b` a later ` price=12` is not detected as a formula (narrow regression from the pin).
- T3.B13 (Editor vendor seams) checker P2: a default-height HTML block whose body sets its own height (600px) gets no Live-badge room, so the badge overlays the bottom of its content (cosmetic; size is correct).
- T3.B7 (DocDO admission revalidation and trash-hold recovery) checker P2: a recovery counts as delivered even when PrincipalDO drops it after a failed credential read — DocDO `#recover` (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts) clears the holders and retry marker once `publishReopened` resolves, but `PrincipalDO.publish` also resolves when its credential read fails (it closes workspace sockets 1013 and sends nothing), and on reconnect the client sees only `{type:'vaults'}`, so a held-out pane stays terminal (downgraded from Codex P1) → have publish report failure so the DocDO keeps the holders and retries.
- T3.B7 (DocDO admission revalidation and trash-hold recovery) checker P2: `MAX_HOLDERS=256` silently drops the oldest holders still owed a recovery — `#keepHolders` truncates with `slice(-MAX_HOLDERS)` and holders refused at admission are recorded without the 50-connection cap, so more than 256 distinct principal/token pairs during one hold can evict an early link-only holder that never hears the recovery (pathological) → bound differently or fall back to a broad recovery when truncated.
- T3.B27 (The full lane fails only on a real regression) checker P2: invariant 3's `socketProblems` (both engines) drops every unheard socket close within 50 ms of its open as a WebKit artifact, so two overlapping doc sockets that each close unheard within 50 ms just before a navigation would go unreported → identify the WebKit artifact positively (e.g. page-side construction evidence) instead of by lifetime.
- T3.B27 (The full lane fails only on a real regression) checker P2: after a failed backlinks refresh (non-404 status or network error) `readBacklinks` keeps the stale cache and `watchBacklinks` reads only for a new note or an empty cache, so reopening or focusing the note no longer retries until another note's workspace event → mark a failed refresh as needing a read.
- T3.B27 (The full lane fails only on a real regression) checker P2: two of the three "WebKit engine crash" classifications are unproven — run 38389781926 (registers `unacked` expected 0, received 1) and run 38401836825 (editor-webkit Run button timeout) log no crash message (only 38416917680 logs "Network process crashed") → keep both open as flakes to diagnose.
- T3.B14 (Media paste, held insertion and media coverage) checker P2: held range ends do not follow text-node splits or merges — `holdEnd` (apps/web/src/host/media/held-insertion.ts:40-59) keeps one Lexical key per end, so a peer formatting the held text mid-upload splits the node and the image lands before the text instead of replacing it; a merge collapses the range to the surviving end (degraded, no data loss) → track ends across splits and merges.
- T3.B14 (Media paste, held insertion and media coverage) checker P2: unmount waits without a deadline for held uploads — `EditorSession.unmountOnce` awaits `surface.settleMedia()` (packages/editor/src/session.ts:987) unbounded, so an asset put or copy that never settles blocks the unmount and its final flush, and `unmount({discardUnsaved})` returns the same pending promise → bound `settleHeldInsertions` with a deadline.
- T3.B14 (Media paste, held insertion and media coverage) checker P2: the B233 viewer range check is not tied to the seek — the snapshot starts with the player, and the test server's 8 KB parts produce ranged reads during the first load, so the test proves ranges are served, not that the seek caused a ranged read (neither engine requests after a seek on the 18 KB clip) → add a seek-driven ranged read check on a clip large enough to force one.
- T3.B14 (Media paste, held insertion and media coverage) checker P2: the j11 editor-link leg covers a no-caret paste, not a paste with an image selected — clicking beside the image leaves no selection, so it exercises the end-of-document fallback; NodeSelection is covered only by a unit test selecting a paragraph, and BUILDPLAN.md:395 overclaims → select the image in the journey leg.
- T3.B14 (Media paste, held insertion and media coverage) checker P2: a refused cross-note image copy still deletes the held selection — in ExternalImagePastePlugin's cross-note path (vendor/.../ExternalImagePastePlugin.tsx:248-286) each copy failure is caught per asset and `$restore` then removes the selected text even when nothing lands (not a regression; B170 triaged P2) → keep the text when no asset lands.
- T3.B8 (Client workspace refresh, leave-abort and bridge inventory) checker P2: create and duplicate show 'Try again' after a leave cut the request off — when beforeunload cuts a create or duplicate and the navigation is then cancelled, sendOnce reconciles the listing and rethrows LeaveCancelled, which moss's App callers treat as failure, though the change may have landed → treat LeaveCancelled as handled in the create and duplicate callers.
- T3.B8 (Client workspace refresh, leave-abort and bridge inventory) checker P2: a visibility probe timer survives an answered pong — after an answered probe, a black-holed socket plus a second visibility change before the first timer fires gets no probe timer of its own, so the 60 s heartbeat check catches the dead socket instead of PROBE_MS → arm a fresh probe timer per probe.
- T3.B8 (Client workspace refresh, leave-abort and bridge inventory) checker P2: askFolderAccess releases the leave before the body is read on the AbortSignal.any fallback only — the read callback checks only the status, so with the listener fallback the leave no longer reaches a still-streaming body (native AbortSignal.any unaffected) → hold the leave through body consumption.
- T3.B8 (Client workspace refresh, leave-abort and bridge inventory) checker P2: some acceptance evidence is unit-level, not journey-level — dropped-publication convergence is covered only by a unit test of the RECONCILE_MS refresh (file-link hover was verified in the checker's browser pass; the j05 negative control is t/T3.B8-negctl3, run 38453911157) → add a journey that drops one meta publication and asserts the sidebar converges.
- T3.B9 (Search index correctness and cost) checker P2: an older successful search feed can clear a newer failed feed's retry — #feedSearch in packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts resets #feedRetryAt and #feedFailures on every success outside the `edits === this.#edits` generation check, and feeds (onSave, payload timer, reindex, alarm) can overlap, so feed A finishing after feed B failed clears B's retry and that revision stays unsearchable until the next edit or wake → guard the reset with the generation check and add an overlapping-feeds test.
- T3.B9 (Search index correctness and cost) checker P2: B173's red-first failure was a mock type error, not a missing backlink — the tests-first commit changed the fake SearchDO.backlinks shape before the route read it, so the red run failed with 'ids.flatMap is not a function' and never reached the 'found on the first read' assertion (the green assertions are meaningful) → no code change; keep red-first mocks shape-compatible with the old route.
- T4.P (m4 on the fixed m3) checker P2: rename validates the open sockets before awaiting the caller's authorization (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:986-987), so a reader revoked during that await can still get the new title if their kick is late; same code on m3, fix there by validating the caller and sockets together.
- T4.P (m4 on the fixed m3) checker P2: j09 reload test (e2e/journeys/j09-revoke-live.spec.ts:189) still flakes in WebKit: removing interception before the last reload lets the last held read finish 200, so only 2 of 3 reads are cancelled; fix in m3 by keeping interception until the old page leaves while letting the new page's requests through.
- T5.P (m5 on the fixed m4) checker P2 (downgraded from Codex P1; same class as B257): #review in packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:1535-1549 validates sockets and credentials before awaiting #resolveActor and does not re-check after, so a reviewer whose session ends, or a reader revoked with a missed kick, during that D1 read can still review or receive the update → pass the actor epoch into #validate as T4.B1 did for comment writes.
- T5.P (m5 on the fixed m4) checker P2: j01-paste-check is close to its 5 s stall bound (5184 ms on attempt 1 of run 38507326778, 4.1-4.4 s on m4 alone in run 38509161402); margin inherited from m4 → m3/m4 paste test follow-up: widen the margin.
- T5.P integration P2: packages/sync/test/harness/suggest-ingest.test.ts:703 ('a maximum-size forged frame ... costs the same') times the runner's wall clock; attempt 1 of full run 38512418550 on the T5.P tree measured 19.28 ms against an 18.32 ms bound (the same tree measured 11.20 ms in run 38507326778) → count work instead of timing, as T3.B27 did for the linear-time tests.
- T6.B3 (Branch predated the m6 head; full lane must rerun on the integrated commit) checker P2: closed at integration; the BUILDPLAN conflict kept both the T6.S5 and T6.B3 lines and the full lane ran on the merged m6 head.
- T6.B3 (History target placed between a comment and the target it describes) checker P2: in `e2e/parity/targets.ts` the `history` entry sits under the comment for the comment-detached target; move it.
- T6.B3 (The History parity reference is a hand-written copy of the view's markup) checker P2: `e2e/parity/oracle/HistoryPage.stories.tsx` copies HistoryView's classes, labels and size, so it is a stored baseline rather than an independent glyphdown derivation; any deliberate History or shell layout change must update the story in the same commit.
- T6.B2 (Move refusal can be bypassed when exact alignment falls back to unique signatures on large duplicate-heavy bodies) checker P2: when align()'s middle table exceeds CHILD_CELL_BUDGET in packages/core/src/reconcile.ts it aligns only unique signatures, so repeated blocks get no exact pairs, crossesExact() puts every copy in gap 0 where counts cancel, and tier 2 pairs same-identity paragraphs by position; about 1,000 alternating A/B paragraphs restored to B/A are rewritten in place, moving anchors and withheld peer inserts to the other block's text instead of being refused.
- T6.B1 (A spill held past its 1 h grace has no sweep protection) checker P2: the sweep skips keys held by a version row or a still-staged version, but not a named or auto spill whose put is still running or a staged move a later restore pruned; an R2 put stalled past SPILL_GRACE_MS lets another pass's sweep delete the key after the save publishes its row. Protect a key from its spill until the version row lands.
- T6.B1 (The failed-auto test does not pin the immediate sweep) checker P2: `versions-lifecycle.test` asserts the failed auto version's cleanup only after wake(), so it still passes if the #versionsChanged() call in the #autoVersion catch is removed; assert the spill is gone before the wake.
- T6.P (onClose compares connection ids, not socket identity, when deciding on the last-disconnect auto version) checker P2: a replacement socket that opens with the same connection id before the stale one closes is left out, so an auto version can be written while a peer is still connected (an early checkpoint, no lost edits); compare with `other !== connection && isOpen(other)` as m5's alarm logic does, and extend the reused-id harness case to assert no version is written.
- T4.B3 (Comment client: draft retention, block comments, highlight paint and hover cost) checker P2: failed replies can come back in reverse submission order across frames — if reply B fails and is offered in one frame and the earlier reply A fails in a later frame, the reply box holds B then A (both texts survive; order is kept only among failures offered in the same frame, and the j15 leg sorts the paragraphs it reads back, so it does not check order).
- T4.B3 (Comment client: draft retention, block comments, highlight paint and hover cost) checker P2: the refusal banner stays up after a successful retry until its 4 s timer expires, because success does not call settleRefusal for the matching refusal (carried-over P2).
- T4.B3 (Comment client: draft retention, block comments, highlight paint and hover cost) checker P2: the block header Add comment button (`data-comment-entry`, a commenter's only enabled code-block header button) has an empty aria-label and no text, so it has no accessible name.
- T3.B10 (Asset upload authority and content checks) checker P2: SVG sniffing rejects pathological-but-valid SVGs (U+FEFF/U+1680 in a namespace prefix; non-IBM037 EBCDIC code pages) — accepted by the coordinator as intended hardening of B166; nothing is released, so no shipped contract narrows.
- T3.B15 (Register and formula collaborative editing) checker P2: astral token markers can collide with literal lone surrogates brought together by concurrent deletions — once every non-surrogate BMP unit is used, createTokenCodec reserves only astral code points already present as complete surrogate pairs, so a lone high and a lone low surrogate that two concurrent deletions bring together can form an assigned marker (e.g. U+F0000) and decode turns that literal text into a reference (needs a formula using every non-surrogate BMP unit plus lone surrogates) → also reserve astral code points a lone high or low surrogate in any source could form.
- T3.B11 (Asset quota, streaming reads and near-cap probe) checker P2: the B219 near-cap probe has no staging run and no Worker memory telemetry — j11-media's SP9 probe runs in CI on the local wrangler stack and records only byte count and upload time, and local workerd does not enforce the 128 MB isolate limit (evidence gap, no demonstrated failure) → add the staging leg with memory and duration telemetry to the m8 canary (T8.2).
- T4.Q (m4 on the m3 fixes) checker P2, owned by m3 (from T3.B10): a duplicate refused after source access is lost returns a different 404 body: `apps/web/src/api/docs.ts:195-198` answers the late refusal (source revoked or trashed while the snapshot is pending) with refuse(404, 'not-found', <message>) while admission uses `notFound()` (respond.ts:8, no message), so the two denials are not byte-identical as A§8 requires (no unauthorized copy).
- T5.Q (m5 on the m4 fixes) checker P2: no single regression test covers an unmounted Suggest-mode preedit — j01 covers unmounting a preedit in Edit mode and j16 covers compositionend in Suggest mode, but nothing exercises the combined unmount flush in register-input.ts (the red on 43da2b70 was a typecheck failure, not behavioral) → add a journey that unmounts an active Suggest-mode preedit while a peer edits, proven red against a draft-client apply.
- T6.Q (No deterministic selftests for the setup auth resend) checker P2: setupPost's resend and mintPrincipal's 'already exists' recovery in e2e/lib/principals.ts have no focused selftests; add cases that an ordinary 503 stays a failure, a non-503 carrying the proxy text stays a failure, a second failed attempt stays a failure, and recovery returns the real user id.
- T7.Q (Two dismissed CI failures have no baseline failure on record) checker P2: of the four failures dismissed in the full-lane reruns, note-store.linear (run 38535601745) and suggest-ingest T5.2 (run 38512418550) failed the same assertions on earlier base runs, but two have no baseline on record: the WebKit j15-comments-drafts uncaught 'ResizeObserver loop' on attempt 1 (comment code and journey identical to m6 1b681ff1; head and m6 both passed 8 of 8 repeats in runs 38717509595 and 38717513000) and suggest.test.ts:791 T5.4 'one fixed frame size in every scenario' (2 sizes instead of 1).
- T7.B3 checker P2 (pull pins its workspace root only after resolveDocId's request): in pull, resolveDocId runs before findRoot/pinRoot (packages/cli/src/program.ts:340-343), so a root swapped for a link during a title-prefix GET /api/docs is pinned as trusted and pull writes the note and state there → select and pin the root before resolveDocId, with a regression test that swaps the root during a title-prefix pull.
- T7.B3 checker P2 (State-file reads and lock retries skip the pinned-root and link re-checks): readBase uses only noLinks, not realInside or the pin, so a base-missing retry can send an outside .moss-multi/<id>/base.md (program.ts:393-395); writeInside calls mkdirSync before its first realInside check, so a swap during add or create can leave an empty outside directory; lockWorkspace re-pins on each retry but runs noLinks once, so a .moss-multi swapped for a link while waiting can get an outside .lock (workspace.ts:436-468).
- T7.B3 checker P2 (Device-login server fields are not field-escaped): deviceLogin builds its CliError from the server's error_description without a status (config.ts:176-192) and the top-level handler applies fieldSafe only when the error has a status (program.ts:593-595), so an LF in that text starts a new output line; user_code is printed only through ttySafe.
- T3.B11 (Asset quota, streaming reads and near-cap probe) re-check P2: SP9 probe records no Worker memory telemetry — j11-media's near-cap probe checks bytes, SHA-256, exact size and the 206 tail read but not Worker memory or 128 MB isolate headroom on deployed staging (same gap as above, already a required test under T8.2).
- T3.B17 (Read-only controls and folder permission UI) checker P2: invariant 9's tab-navigation exemption also applies outside read-only bodies — the detector exempts any [role=tablist] > div[role=tab] under any non-live title or body binding without checking data-body-binding=readonly, so a focusable tab in an unbound or terminal body or a closed title passes; limit the exemption to a read-only body binding and add negative selftest fixtures for unbound and terminal bodies (test gate only; no product defect).
- T4.Q second pass (m4 on the later m3 fixes) checker P2, owned by m3 (from T3.B17): invariant 9's tab exemption in e2e/lib/detectors.js editableUnbound is not limited to readonly body bindings, so a focused tab in an unbound or terminal body goes unreported (same item as the T3.B17 line above; not introduced by the merge).
- T5.Q second pass (m5 on the later m4 fixes) checker P2: j01-paste-check stall flake is closer to the bound than reported — attempts 1 and 2 of run 39200483704 failed e2e (chromium, pasterace) at e2e/lib/paste.ts:294, attempt 1 at 5395 ms against a 5112 ms bound, and t/T7.P2 failed it at 5029 ms (run 39181829307), so the timing sensitivity spans branches → T3.B27's calibrated stall bounds reaching m5 is the stated fix (same item as the T5.S7 and T5.P lines).
- T5.Q second pass (m5 on the later m4 fixes) checker P2, owned by m3 (from T3.B17): invariant 9's tab exemption (e2e/lib/detectors.js:91-100) covers every non-live binding, not only readonly bodies (same item as the T3.B17 line above; not introduced by the merge).
- T5.S10 (Suggest-mode paste is Lexical's own) checker P2: typing after an undo, redo and second undo of an Enter brings the restored rest back as a suggestion → T5.S14 red-first line.
- T5.S14 (Accept never applies an identity-only change) checker P2: an unsaved deletion inside a restored list item is not in the offered text after a kept accept (coordinator override: nothing written is lost; the offered text holds the pre-deletion content).
- T5.S14 (Accept never applies an identity-only change) checker P2: addendum 3 item 1 narrowed by coordinator ruling: shown text lands, unshown text is buried (recorded in the brief).
- T5.S14 (Accept never applies an identity-only change) checker P2: the deleted-anchor 409 is tested only through the relocation helper.
- T5.S14 (Accept never applies an identity-only change) checker P2: after a kept accept halts input, the suggester pane hides the restored item until reload.
- T5.B2 (Suggest-mode routing, rebuild provenance and pending ownership) checker P2: B300/B301 provenance still comes from matching text values (trace.ts sliceOf maps offsets only when the new text is one unique slice of the old), so a long rewrite that also unescapes text falls past ALIGN_CAP and drops the strike silently (routing.ts:174, 425-430), and an ambiguous serif-wrapper match can align the wrong character → carry the transform's real source offsets.
- T5.B2 (Suggest-mode routing, rebuild provenance and pending ownership) checker P2: B302 struck text rebuilt alone at the start of a block draws no ghost: placeOf (paint.ts:369-398) searches only leftward, so with no anchor ghostsOf drops it (e.g. strike the first x of 'x tail x.' and highlight only that x).
- T5.B2 (Suggest-mode routing, rebuild provenance and pending ownership) checker P2: B302 adjacent ghost characters are joined in id order (removedStrikes sorts by client and clock, client.ts:795), not document order, so a run created out of order reads 'bx' instead of 'xb'.
- T5.B2 (Suggest-mode routing, rebuild provenance and pending ownership) checker P2: emptiedMaps walks left past tombstones for each removed id (client.ts:345-351), an unmeasured O(N^2) cost on the editing path for a densely struck text node.
- T5.B3 (Suggestion paint, preview layout and Suggest-mode formulas) checker P2: reopened-preview pointermove listeners are not disposed on redraw or unmount; each redraw that keeps a preview open adds a document-level listener that removes itself only on a later pointer move, so a stationary pointer during a peer's typing accumulates listeners and keeps detached wedges in memory (bounded, not data loss). Closed by T9.55d: a repaint keeps the wedge, so the reopen path and its listeners are gone.
- T5.B3 (Suggestion paint, preview layout and Suggest-mode formulas) checker P2: Review-mode paintBound rebuilds the SpanSet on every repaint because Review mode passes partTargets(...) as an array, so each selection or resize repaint re-sorts and re-merges the strike index (O(S log S) per paint).
- T5.B3 (Suggestion paint, preview layout and Suggest-mode formulas) checker P2: a suggested formula paragraph has an empty wedge label ('Suggested insert: ') and a '(new block)' preview in Edit mode, and its card lists raw formulaId and regId hashes (cosmetic, newly reachable since B306).
- T5.B4 (Suggestion apply, describe and copy correctness and cost) checker P2: Copy suggested text splits proposed text at inline links: walkBody flushes the run entering and leaving every non-Map ContentType, including a link's nested Y.XmlText, so 'See [site](url) now.' copies as 'See \nsite\n now.' (copy-text.test.ts has no link case).
- T5.B4 (Suggestion apply, describe and copy correctness and cost) checker P2: the fallback for typed text that no longer lands appends ContentString in typing order, not document order, and drops linebreak and tab nodes, so 'tail' then a prepended 'head' in a deleted, GC'd paragraph copies as 'tailhead'.
- T3.B4 (Sync session liveness, paste admission and answer ordering) checker P2: a reconnect usage figure marks unsent payload writes as counted — `AckLedger.usageReported` (apps/web/src/host/collab/acks.ts:70-80) marks every pending payload write as counted, but the DocDO sends 'usage' on connect (doc-do.ts:483-489) before the replayed writes land, so the paste check can briefly undercount payload growth made while disconnected; the DocDO still enforces the cap.
- T3.B4 (Sync session liveness, paste admission and answer ordering) checker P2: splitAnswer spreads unbounded lists into push() — packages/sync/src/update-pieces.ts:168 `ready.push(...woken.filter(...))` and :173 `ordered.push(...run.slice(...))` can pass the engine's argument limit on a crafted answer with a large client-id fan-out; owned by T3.B28.
- T3.B30 (An upload's memory follows the bytes it has sent) checker P2: the unit test for a disconnect never starts a real streamed R2 put — the recorded-bucket helper in apps/web/src/api/assets.test.ts:868-876 cancels the readable and throws before target.put (:878), so the null head assertion (:917) comes from the helper rather than an interrupted native put and would still pass if putParts stopped aborting its writer; no production defect (no-multipart and quota-retry assertions remain valid).
- T3.B28 (splitAnswer is linear and stack-safe) checker P2: the 'at the cap' fixture is about 2 MB of encoded state, not the DocDO state cap — the harness and unit tests seed interleavedChains(2000, 120000) (~2 MB), while STATE_CAP_BYTES is ~26 MiB (2 MiB markdown cap x STATE_RATIO x 1.25); a fixture sized to stateCapBytes would make the boundary explicit (coverage gap only; the algorithm is linear).
- T4.U (m4 on m3's sync and upload fixes) checker P2: intermittent WebKit j03-connection socket-count failure still unexplained — in attempt 1 of run 39425153421, 'a refused write rebinds fresh and a deleted doc locks in place' failed with Ben opening 3 doc sockets where 2 are allowed; it passed on rerun and in an exact-head WebKit repeat_each=3 run (39429710184); root cause and whether base lanes show it are not established (the merge changed only tests after T3.B4; related Chromium flake logged under T1.S1).
- T3.B31b (A note stores only what differs from the defaults) checker P2: a tab on the earlier build that undoes while offline can store a NaN indent that every peer then keeps; older tabs are refused by the client protocol gate (owned by T8.V) and the wrong-type value is repaired by T3.B33.
- T3.B31b (A note stores only what differs from the defaults) checker P2: the server's default write-back competes with a concurrent user write by client id — lean-defaults.ts:85-87 writes an ordinary map value, so a peer setting the same key at the same moment can lose to the server's default; owned by T3.B33.
- T3.B31b (A note stores only what differs from the defaults) checker P2: on the earlier build, redoing a first write does nothing after the server's write-back (the write-back is a later remote change, so Yjs refuses that client's redo of its first bold, color, alignment or indent; peers still converge); A§10.11 statement owned by T3.B33.
- T3.B31b (A note stores only what differs from the defaults) checker P2: default write-backs skip the state-cap admission check — the repair transaction is written after #overCap admitted only the incoming frame (doc-do.ts:683-695; admission.ts:179-187), so a small frame that deletes many keys can push a doc near its cap over it; owned by T3.B33.
- T3.B31c (The note size limit matches Google Docs and holds on the real server) checker P2: payload map property writes get only the deletion headroom, not the format/property headroom — the payload-frame path (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts:1606) passes !growth.inserts to #limitMet while the body path passes !growth.content (:1508), so at STRUCT_CAP a keyed-property write on a chart or sketch payload is refused; chart and sketch map keys are content, so any headroom must not let payload content grow past the cap.
- T3.B31c (The note size limit matches Google Docs and holds on the real server) checker P2: a paste flushed or timed out while named payloads are still arriving is checked with incomplete counts — payloadsArrive (apps/web/src/host/large-paste.ts:207-212) stops on a flush or after 10 s without reporting readiness and landPaste (:865-866) then checks the local payload counts, so the server can refuse such a paste partway; when the wait ends while awaiting() is still non-empty, the preflight should refuse or re-check rather than admit.
- T3.B31c (The note size limit matches Google Docs and holds on the real server) checker P2: the refusal band does not look like Moss's error notice — compared with Moss's retry toast (story retry-toast--save-failed) it is a flat full-width strip with neutral colours and no warning icon, not a rounded floating toast with Moss's error colours; pre-existing styling outside this task, which changed only the wording.
- T3.B33 (The note server never keeps a body property of the wrong type) checker P2: a valid write that loses to a concurrent invalid write ends at the default — key X; peer A (client 100) writes NaN after X; peer B (client 50), not having seen A, writes indent 2 after X; the server repairs A to 0 under client 0, then B arrives and the value is 0, not 2; Yjs Item.integrate orders B before A by client id with or without the repair (plain Yjs would end at NaN), so B loses to A's invalid write, not to the repair.
- T3.B33 (The note server never keeps a body property of the wrong type) checker P2: keepLeanKeys walks every client in transaction.afterState per transaction before skipping unchanged ones, outside repairWork.steps, so the cost is O(historical clients + touched structs) rather than O(keys touched) and the equal-steps test cannot see it; cheap per client and bounded in practice.
- T3.B33 (The note server never keeps a body property of the wrong type) checker P2: the BUILDPLAN line names an 'offline indent, undo, indent' undo-lean test that does not exist — the two-peer test injects NaN directly into the earlier binding's Y.Doc and then indents and outdents through setIndent, because on m3 that sequence never produced NaN; the test is real and was red first, but the BUILDPLAN line overstates it.
- T3.B32 (A paste that cannot finish leaves nothing behind) checker P2: the cut notice first says 'nothing was pasted', then changes to the 'kept' wording — while the note is hidden and rebinding the cutting tab shows "The paste didn't fit, so nothing was pasted.", and once the rollback finds a line someone else edited it changes to "...It was removed, except for what someone else had already edited."; the body is hidden during the first text and the final text is correct, so this is a brief wording flicker, not a wrong result.
- T4.V (m4 on m3's note size limits) checker P2, owned by m3 (from T3.B31c): a paste that replaces a selection counts the selected text twice — pasteLimit in apps/web/src/host/large-paste.ts adds the whole rehearsed paste to the live character and struct counts without subtracting the selection it replaces, so a fitting replacement paste near TEXT_CHAR_CAP can be refused (refuses only; never loses data; code identical to m3).
- T4.V (m4 on m3's note size limits) checker P2: a reply reserves two structs although it writes one — createComment always checks COMMENT_STRUCTS=2 while the reply branch of comments.create writes a single c:<id> record, so with exactly one struct left a reply is refused with 413 'structure' (off by one, conservative).
- T4.V (m4 on m3's note size limits) checker P2, owned by m3 (duplicate path): duplicate admission leaves out the replacement title's structs — createFromSnapshot admits the snapshot and payload growth, then writes the new title uncounted, so a snapshot that exactly fills the structure guard can end a few structs over it.
- T4.V (m4 on m3's note size limits) checker P2: anchor re-mints flushed after an admitted frame are not counted against the structure guard — the comments flush can write changed anchor records that admission never reserved; the 2,000-record cap bounds the live count but not the accumulated history.
- T5.U (m5 on m4's sync and upload fixes) checker P2: WebKit j00-import tables.md invariant 5 intermittently fails with a table column resize handle covering the canvas at 852,947 (1 in 3 on the T5.U head and on each parent, m5 and m4); e2e/lib/detectors.js:21-45 rejects handles mounted on the body while vendor TableColumnResizePlugin.tsx:713-772 portals them there.
- T5.U (m5 on m4's sync and upload fixes) checker P2: timing budgets are tight: the j01-paste-check stall failed once at 5320 ms against 5300 ms and the converter's entity-tabs import at 260 ms against 250 ms (reruns 4002 ms and 230 ms; parents show the same variance).
- T6.U (m6 on m5's sync and upload fixes) checker P2, owned by m5: the suggestion checkpoint cache is not bounded by bytes — Composite.build keeps a full encoded document in each valid record's Step and retains every Step, so a 5 MiB note with 100 open records could hold about 500 MiB (static reading; no OOM reproduced).
- T6.U (m6 on m5's sync and upload fixes) checker P2, owned by m5: ghostsOf rescans the removed strike prefix for each ghost character — placeOf walks back through the gone set per removed character, quadratic on long struck runs (up to 20,000 items; static reading, not measured).
- T6.U (m6 on m5's sync and upload fixes) checker P2: j01-paste-check (chromium) stall budget flakes at e2e/lib/paste.ts:294 (5634>5212 and 5083>5000 ms during moss-paste-batch layout, 2 of 3 repeats on 8b49e971); base m6 06591a08 fails it 2 of 3 too (run 39522279454), while the merge head passed 3 of 3 (run 39522281687) and m5 104d4cce 3 of 3 (run 39522277036) — a pre-existing m6 flake that needs its own fix (same family as the T5.Q second pass and T5.U lines; T3.B27's calibrated stall bounds).
- T5.S15 (A Review view left open across a kept accept shows the note as it now is) checker P2: the kept-accept remount does not keep a long note's Review scroll position; apps/web/src/host/collab/suggest/mounts.ts:250-256 sends a kept accept through PaneBinding.#remount (apps/web/src/host/collab/pane.tsx:249-258, 393-401, 528-538), which saves only a caret, resets the root and opens a new session, and the vendored CanvasAreaContent scroll restore runs only when the note id changes; the same pre-existing path a non-accepted gone record already takes.
- T5.V (m5 on m4's note size limits) checker P2: undo of a striking paste can land in part at the structure limit and its history is then used up; an edge at the structure guard in Suggest mode where the server never exceeds a limit and the refusal is shown with the unsaved text offered back.
- T5.V (m5 on m4's note size limits) checker P2: the refusal band is a square, full-width strip with only a bottom border, not Moss's floating, rounded error toast with a full outline (1440 and 390, light and dark); colours, text size and weight match Moss, and on a phone the actions wrap under the message.
- T5.V (m5 on m4's note size limits) checker P2, owned by m3 (from T3.B31c): a refused paste's size-limit notice under the top bar is a neutral white row with dark text and a neutral rule, not error-styled like Moss's red text on a pale red surface with a red border.
- T4.W (m4 with m3's paste rollback) checker P2: the moves ownership check looks only at the destination's endpoints and any of the socket's writes — #ownMoves in packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts keeps a move when its first and last items fall in clock ranges that socket wrote, without checking the interior or that the writes came from the paste, so a malformed client could land a comment on an occurrence whose middle is another writer's text.
- T4.W (m4 with m3's paste rollback) checker P2: reconnect replay, step 2 and stored whole-paste recovery send a paste's held deletes without the moves hint (doc-session.ts sends it only at release), and the server's per-socket written record resets on reconnect, so if the release is lost the comment shows as detached (text survives).
- T4.W (m4 with m3's paste rollback) checker P2, owned by m3: the memory job 'structure cap: the largest table' is unstable near its budget — its save step measured 87 MB against 86.4 MB on two attempts and 85 MB on the third, matching 85 MB on the m4 and m3 bases; make the job steadier.
- T5.W (m5 with m4's paste rollback) checker P2, pre-existing: the paste rollback notice ("The paste didn't fit, so nothing was pasted.") uses the plain full-width notice band in apps/web/src/host/surfaces/RefusalAnnouncer.tsx, not Moss's error notice style (retry-toast--save-failed: centered rounded card, red text on pale red, border, shadow, warning icon).
- T5.W (m5 with m4's paste rollback) checker P2: no direct test for a role change while a large paste is landing (editor demoted to suggester, or suggester promoted to editor); the paths (#dropRollbacks on demotion, trackPaste resolving only the session body, fork pastes skipping batching) were checked by reading only.
- T6.V (m6 on m5's note size limits) checker P2: a restore's comment re-anchor writes are not counted by its admission — restoreVersion admits only the reconciled diff and payloads (packages/sync/src/doc-do.ts), then comments.flush() and reanchor() write anchor records unadmitted, so a restore that exactly fills STRUCT_CAP can end a few structs over it (bounded by COMMENTS_PER_DOC, inside STRUCT_CAP's memory margin; a later growing restore is refused).
- T6.V (m6 on m5's note size limits) checker P2, pre-existing (also at T4.V): duplicate checks the source's bytes, not the finished copy's — createFromSnapshot checks the source snapshot and payload bytes against stateCapBytes, then deletes the destination seed and writes the ' copy' title uncounted (overshoot of tens of bytes, reachable only in a corner).
- T6.V (m6 on m5's note size limits) checker P2: after a refused restore the History diff summary is stale — a restore refused because another tab pasted 650,000 characters still showed the summary loaded before the paste; the refusal does not reload the current note (refusal text correct, nothing lost).
